The palette names aged warm-tea silk ground, soot-ink and dark blue-black lines, persimmon and ochre in skin warmth and the discarded jacket, malachite and flower green in the begonia leaves and rush mat edge, sandalwood brown and muted indigo in stone and textiles, with restrained shell-white on exposed skin and powdered-gold accent on the basin rim; contour-enclosed color fields keep each shape flat, the three bounded skin tones read as shell-white base, transparent warm wash at breasts and kneecaps and palm centers, and narrow line-wash beneath her lifted chin and his forearm overlap.
